+  /** 显式删除对话：复用驱动删除文件和目录项，只在成功后清理界面状态。 */
+  async deleteSession(target: WorkspaceSessionTarget): Promise<DesktopAppState> {
+    await this.initialize();
+    const sessionRef = toSessionRef(target);
+    const session = this.sessionFromState(sessionRef);
+    if (!session) return this.withError("该对话已不存在，请刷新列表。");
+    if (session.status === "running") {
+      return this.withError("该对话正在执行，请先停止，再删除对话。");
+    }
+    if (
+      this.state.scheduledTasks.some(
+        (task) =>
+          task.target.kind === "existing-thread" &&
+          task.target.workspaceId === target.workspaceId &&
+          task.target.sessionId === target.sessionId &&
+          task.status !== "completed",
+      )
+    ) {
+      return this.withError("该对话仍绑定定时任务，请先删除对应定时任务。");
+    }
+    return this.withErrorHandling(async () => {
+      await this.cancelPendingDialogsForSession(sessionRef);
+      await this.driver.deleteSession(sessionRef);
+      this.clearPendingAutoTitle(sessionRef);
+      this.unpinSession(sessionRef);
+      this.sessionState.ephemeralForBySession.delete(sessionKey(sessionRef));
+      return this.refreshState(
+        selectionAfterArchiving(
+          {
+            selectedWorkspaceId: this.state.selectedWorkspaceId,
+            selectedSessionId: this.state.selectedSessionId,
+            workspaces: this.state.workspaces,
+          },
+          target,
+        ),
+      );
+    });
+  }

+const SIDE_CHAT_ID_MAX_LENGTH = 256;
+const SIDE_CHAT_TEXT_MAX_LENGTH = 100_000;
+
+function expectSideChatId(value: unknown, name: string): string {
+  const id = expectNonEmptyString(value, name);
+  if (id.length > SIDE_CHAT_ID_MAX_LENGTH) {
+    throw new TypeError(`${name} must be at most ${SIDE_CHAT_ID_MAX_LENGTH} characters`);
+  }
+  return id;
+}
+
+function expectSideChatSourceFields(record: Record<string, unknown>, name: string): SessionRef {
+  return {
+    workspaceId: expectSideChatId(record.workspaceId, `${name}.workspaceId`),
+    sessionId: expectSideChatId(record.sessionId, `${name}.sessionId`),
+  };
+}
+
+/** 辅助对话来源只收已登记会话标识；未知字段在进入宿主前拒绝。 */
+export function expectSideChatRequest(value: unknown): SessionRef {
+  const record = expectRecord(value, "side chat request");
+  rejectUnexpectedFields(record, ["workspaceId", "sessionId"], "side chat request");
+  return expectSideChatSourceFields(record, "side chat request");
+}
+
+/** 辅助对话消息没有附件或控制参数；空白和超长文本都明确拒绝。 */
+export function expectSendSideChatMessageRequest(value: unknown): SendSideChatMessageInput {
+  const record = expectRecord(value, "side chat message");
+  rejectUnexpectedFields(record, ["workspaceId", "sessionId", "text"], "side chat message");
+  const text = expectString(record.text, "side chat message.text");
+  if (!text.trim()) {
+    throw new TypeError("side chat message.text must not be empty");
+  }
+  if (text.length > SIDE_CHAT_TEXT_MAX_LENGTH) {
+    throw new TypeError(
+      `side chat message.text must be at most ${SIDE_CHAT_TEXT_MAX_LENGTH} characters`,
+    );
+  }
+  return {
+    source: expectSideChatSourceFields(record, "side chat message"),
+    text,
+  };
+}

+export function expectWorkboardSessionInput(value: unknown): WorkboardSessionInput {
+  const input = expectRecord(value, "workboard session");
+  const workspaceId = expectNonEmptyString(input.workspaceId, "input.workspaceId");
+  const offset = expectOptionalNonNegativeInteger(input.offset, "input.offset");
+  if (input.kind === "worker")

+/**
+ * 是不是「重载当前页面」：同源同路径（hash/search 差异也算重载，如 dev 的 #/route）。
+ * 用于在重载前先让渲染层交出还在防抖窗口里的草稿——重载会丢掉整个渲染进程。
+ */
+function isPageReload(contentsUrl: string, targetUrl: string): boolean {
+  try {
+    if (!isInAppNavigationUrl(targetUrl)) return false;
+    const current = new URL(contentsUrl);
+    const target = new URL(targetUrl);
+    return current.origin === target.origin && current.pathname === target.pathname;
+  } catch {
+    return false;
+  }
+}

+  // Reload discards the renderer, and with it any draft still inside its debounce window
+  // (typing schedules the save ~350ms out). Hold the reload until the renderer has handed
+  // over its pending drafts, the same way window close does, then reload for real.
+  let reloadAfterDraftFlush = false;
+  window.webContents.on("did-start-navigation", (event) => {
+    if (!event.isMainFrame || reloadAfterDraftFlush) return;
+    // A same-document navigation keeps the renderer, so there is nothing to save.
+    if (event.isSameDocument) return;
+    if (!isPageReload(window.webContents.getURL(), event.url)) return;
+    event.preventDefault();
+    void composerDraftFlusher
+      .flush([window])
+      .then(() => {
+        if (window.isDestroyed() || window.webContents.isDestroyed()) return;
+        reloadAfterDraftFlush = true;
+        window.webContents.reload();
+      })
+      .catch((error: unknown) => {
+        console.error("[main] Draft flush before reload failed", error);
+      });
+  });

+// 驱动包按需加载单点。`import()` 的 specifier 是字面量，打包器能静态识别并保留为外部依赖，
+// 不需要额外静态 import 才能进产物（曾误判过：当时真正的缺陷是「进入部门」按钮只切不读，
+// 与本处写法无关）。加载器按名缓存整模块，避免每个桥各解析一次。
 type Driver = typeof import("@pi-gui/pi-sdk-driver");
 /** fork 构建产物按 `load*` 命名的惰性加载函数集合（驱动包自身导出已带类型）。 */
 type DriverLoaderName = Extract<keyof Driver, `load${string}`>;
 
+/**
+ * 加载器按名缓存整模块（主进程 CJS，不能写顶层 await）：每个 `load*` 名字只解析一次。
+ */
+const driverModule = (): Promise<Driver> => import("@pi-gui/pi-sdk-driver");
+const drivers = new Map<DriverLoaderName, Promise<unknown>>();
+
 /**
  * fork 驱动惰性动态加载单点：各桥只在这里 `await import` 一次，按名取加载函数。
  * 单测不走这里（各桥经 options 注入替身），注入面不变。
  */
 export async function loadDriver<K extends DriverLoaderName>(name: K): Promise<Driver[K]> {
-  return (await import("@pi-gui/pi-sdk-driver"))[name];
+  const cached = drivers.get(name);
+  if (cached) return cached as Promise<Driver[K]>;
+  const loaded = driverModule().then((module) => module[name]);
+  drivers.set(name, loaded);
+  return loaded as Promise<Driver[K]>;
 }

+/** 审核回边是可选展示元数据；坏行过滤，旧内核缺字段时保持 undefined。 */
+function normalizeReviewEdges(
+  value: unknown,
+): { target: string; limit: number; overflowAction: "停滞" | "修完这轮放行" }[] | undefined {
+  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return undefined;
+  const edges = value.flatMap((item) => {
+    if (!item || typeof item !== "object" || Array.isArray(item)) return [];
+    const record = item as Record<string, unknown>;
+    const target = asText(record["target"]).trim();
+    const limit = record["limit"];
+    const overflowAction = record["overflowAction"];
+    if (
+      !target ||
+      typeof limit !== "number" ||
+      !Number.isSafeInteger(limit) ||
+      limit < 0 ||
+      (overflowAction !== "停滞" && overflowAction !== "修完这轮放行")
+    )
+      return [];
+    const action: "停滞" | "修完这轮放行" = overflowAction;
+    return [{ target, limit, overflowAction: action }];
+  });
+  return edges.length > 0 ? edges : undefined;
+}
+
+/** 并行组策略是只读显示元数据；坏组过滤，旧配方缺字段时保持 undefined。 */
+function normalizeParallelGroups(value: unknown): CanvasParallelGroup[] | undefined {
+  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return undefined;
+  const groups = value.flatMap((item) => {
+    if (!item || typeof item !== "object" || Array.isArray(item)) return [];
+    const record = item as Record<string, unknown>;
+    const id = asText(record["id"]).trim();
+    if (!id) return [];
+    const maxConcurrency = record["maxConcurrency"];
+    const failurePolicy = record["failurePolicy"];
+    const cancelPolicy = record["cancelPolicy"];
+    if (
+      maxConcurrency !== undefined &&
+      (typeof maxConcurrency !== "number" ||
+        !Number.isSafeInteger(maxConcurrency) ||
+        maxConcurrency < 1)
+    )
+      return [];
+    if (
+      failurePolicy !== undefined &&
+      failurePolicy !== "fail_fast" &&
+      failurePolicy !== "allow_partial" &&
+      failurePolicy !== "any_success"
+    )
+      return [];
+    if (
+      cancelPolicy !== undefined &&
+      cancelPolicy !== "cancel_pending" &&
+      cancelPolicy !== "drain_started"
+    )
+      return [];
+    const normalizedFailurePolicy: CanvasParallelGroup["failurePolicy"] =
+      failurePolicy === "fail_fast" ||
+      failurePolicy === "allow_partial" ||
+      failurePolicy === "any_success"
+        ? failurePolicy
+        : undefined;
+    const normalizedCancelPolicy: CanvasParallelGroup["cancelPolicy"] =
+      cancelPolicy === "cancel_pending" || cancelPolicy === "drain_started"
+        ? cancelPolicy
+        : undefined;
+    const group: CanvasParallelGroup = {
+      id,
+      ...(maxConcurrency !== undefined ? { maxConcurrency } : {}),
+      ...(normalizedFailurePolicy !== undefined ? { failurePolicy: normalizedFailurePolicy } : {}),
+      ...(normalizedCancelPolicy !== undefined ? { cancelPolicy: normalizedCancelPolicy } : {}),
+    };
+    return [group];
+  });
+  return groups.length > 0 ? groups : undefined;
+}

 /** 节点状态色板（未落卡灰/进行中蓝/已交付绿/人工门等待黄/挂起橙/读取异常红）。 */
 export type CanvasNodeTone = "grey" | "blue" | "green" | "yellow" | "orange" | "red";
@@ -26,9 +27,444 @@ export interface CanvasNodeView {
   /** 卡现读状态；模板节点（未跑实例）缺省＝无卡灰。 */
   readonly status?: string;
   readonly cardRef?: string;
+  readonly cards?: readonly {
+    readonly cardRef: string;
+    readonly status: string;
+    readonly error?: string;
+  }[];

+  readonly visualGroupLabel?: string;
+  readonly visualGroupKind?: "stage" | "parallel" | "condition";

+export type CanvasNodeFilter = "all" | "attention" | "running" | "completed" | "pending";
+
+/** 搜索始终作用于真实节点，包含已经折叠的分组成员。 */
+export function filterCanvasNodes(
+  nodes: readonly CanvasNodeView[],
+  query: string,
+  filter: CanvasNodeFilter,
+): CanvasNodeView[] {
+  const text = query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
+  return nodes.filter((node) => {
+    const tone = canvasNodeTone(node);
+    const matchesStatus =
+      filter === "all" ||
+      (filter === "attention" && needsCanvasAttention(node)) ||
+      (filter === "running" && tone === "blue") ||
+      (filter === "completed" && tone === "green") ||
+      (filter === "pending" && tone === "grey");
+    return (
+      matchesStatus &&
+      (!text ||
+        [
+          node.name,
+          node.dept,
+          node.cardRef,
+          ...(node.cards ?? []).map((card) => card.cardRef),
+          node.suspended,
+          node.status ? cardStatusLabel(node.status) : "",
+        ]
+          .filter(Boolean)
+          .join(" ")
+          .toLocaleLowerCase()
+          .includes(text))
+    );
+  });
+}
+
+/** 正常等待前置或落卡不要求人工处理，避免异常定位被整条等待链淹没。 */
+export function needsCanvasAttention(node: CanvasNodeView): boolean {
+  return (
+    node.unmapped === true ||
+    node.status === "failed" ||
+    node.status === "读取异常" ||
+    (node.requiresConfirmation && !node.confirmation && node.status === "delivered") ||
+    Boolean(node.suspended && !/^(等待前置|等待落卡|等待绑定)/.test(node.suspended))
+  );
+}
+
+/** 专注视图沿上下游分别扩展，避免沿汇聚点反查时误把旁支当成上游。 */
+export function focusedCanvasNodes(
+  nodes: readonly CanvasNodeView[],
+  selected: string,
+  depth = 1,
+): CanvasNodeView[] {
+  const current = nodes.find((node) => node.name === selected);
+  if (!current) return [...nodes];
+  const names = new Set([
+    selected,
+    ...current.dependsOn,
+    ...(current.reviewEdges ?? []).map((edge) => edge.target),
+  ]);
+  const byName = new Map(nodes.map((node) => [node.name, node]));
+  const downstream = new Map<string, string[]>();
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    for (const dependency of node.dependsOn) {
+      const children = downstream.get(dependency) ?? [];
+      children.push(node.name);
+      downstream.set(dependency, children);
+    }
+  }
+  for (const direction of ["up", "down"] as const) {
+    const visited = new Set([selected]);
+    let frontier = [selected];
+    for (let level = 0; level < depth && frontier.length > 0; level++) {
+      const next: string[] = [];
+      for (const name of frontier) {
+        const neighbours = direction === "up" ? byName.get(name)?.dependsOn : downstream.get(name);
+        for (const neighbour of neighbours ?? []) {
+          if (visited.has(neighbour)) continue;
+          visited.add(neighbour);
+          names.add(neighbour);
+          next.push(neighbour);
+        }
+      }
+      frontier = next;
+    }
+  }
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    if (node.reviewEdges?.some((edge) => edge.target === selected)) names.add(node.name);
+  }
+  return nodes.filter((node) => names.has(node.name));
+}
+
+export interface CanvasOverviewGroup {
+  readonly id: string;
+  readonly label: string;
+  readonly kind: "stage" | "parallel" | "condition";
+  readonly members: readonly CanvasNodeView[];
+  readonly summary: string;
+  readonly tone: CanvasNodeTone;
+}
+
+export interface CanvasOverviewItem {
+  readonly id: string;
+  readonly view: CanvasNodeView;
+  readonly group?: CanvasOverviewGroup;
+}
+
+export interface CanvasOverviewGraph {
+  readonly items: readonly CanvasOverviewItem[];
+  readonly edges: readonly CanvasLayoutEdge[];
+  readonly groups: readonly CanvasOverviewGroup[];
+}
+
+export const COMPACT_OVERVIEW_NODE_COUNT = 8;
+
+/** 大图或收起后的单条主干按行折返；分支图保留 DAG，不猜执行顺序。 */
+export function overviewChainOrder(graph: CanvasOverviewGraph): readonly string[] | undefined {
+  if (
+    graph.items.length < 4 ||
+    (graph.items.length < COMPACT_OVERVIEW_NODE_COUNT && !graph.items.some((item) => item.group))
+  )
+    return undefined;
+  const incoming = new Map(graph.items.map((item) => [item.id, 0]));
+  const next = new Map<string, string>();
+  for (const edge of graph.edges) {
+    if (edge.back) continue;
+    if (next.has(edge.from)) return undefined;
+    next.set(edge.from, edge.to);
+    const count = (incoming.get(edge.to) ?? 0) + 1;
+    if (count > 1) return undefined;
+    incoming.set(edge.to, count);
+  }
+  const starts = [...incoming].filter(([, count]) => count === 0);
+  if (starts.length !== 1) return undefined;
+  const order: string[] = [];
+  const visited = new Set<string>();
+  let current: string | undefined = starts[0]?.[0];
+  while (current !== undefined && !visited.has(current)) {
+    visited.add(current);
+    order.push(current);
+    current = next.get(current);
+  }
+  return order.length === graph.items.length ? order : undefined;
+}
+
+function groupTone(members: readonly CanvasNodeView[]): CanvasNodeTone {
+  const tones = members.map(overviewNodeTone);
+  if (tones.includes("red")) return "red";
+  if (tones.includes("orange")) return "orange";
+  if (tones.includes("yellow")) return "yellow";
+  if (tones.includes("blue")) return "blue";
+  if (tones.length > 0 && tones.every((tone) => tone === "green")) return "green";
+  return "grey";
+}
+
+/** 读取异常是数据可读性问题，不能在压缩组摘要里冒充失败节点。 */
+function overviewNodeTone(node: CanvasNodeView): CanvasNodeTone {
+  return node.status === "读取异常" ? "grey" : canvasNodeTone(node);
+}
+
+function groupSummary(members: readonly CanvasNodeView[]): string {
+  const counts = new Map<CanvasNodeTone, number>();
+  const readErrorCount = members.filter((member) => member.status === "读取异常").length;
+  for (const member of members) {
+    const tone = overviewNodeTone(member);
+    counts.set(tone, (counts.get(tone) ?? 0) + 1);
+  }
+  const parts = [`${members.length} 个节点`];
+  const branches = new Set(
+    members
+      .map((member) => member.branchId?.trim())
+      .filter((branch): branch is string => Boolean(branch)),
+  );
+  if (branches.size > 0) parts.push(`${branches.size} 条分支`);
+  const labels: readonly [CanvasNodeTone, string][] = [
+    ["green", "已完成"],
+    ["blue", "进行中"],
+    ["yellow", "待确认"],
+    ["orange", "已挂起"],
+    ["red", "失败"],
+    ["grey", "待处理"],
+  ];
+  for (const [tone, label] of labels) {
+    // 读取异常不是执行失败；从灰色“待处理”里拆出来，避免摘要把坏读档
+    // 显示成“失败 N”，同时保留普通未落卡节点的待处理计数。
+    const count = tone === "grey" ? (counts.get(tone) ?? 0) - readErrorCount : counts.get(tone);
+    if (count) parts.push(`${label} ${count}`);
+  }
+  if (readErrorCount > 0) parts.push(`读取异常 ${readErrorCount}`);
+  return parts.join(" · ");
+}
+
+function makeGroup(
+  id: string,
+  label: string,
+  kind: CanvasOverviewGroup["kind"],
+  members: readonly CanvasNodeView[],
+): CanvasOverviewGroup {
+  return { id, label, kind, members, summary: groupSummary(members), tone: groupTone(members) };
+}
+
+/**
+ * 把 DAG 压成安全的树状展示投影：显式 visualGroupId 优先，其余只压缩至少 3 个节点的
+ * 单进单出线性段。投影只改变画布显示，原始节点和边始终保留在详细图中。
+ */
+export function buildCanvasOverview(
+  nodes: readonly CanvasNodeView[],
+  expandedGroups: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(),
+): CanvasOverviewGraph {
+  const byName = new Map(nodes.map((node) => [node.name, node]));
+  const incoming = new Map<string, number>();
+  const outgoing = new Map<string, string[]>();
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    incoming.set(node.name, 0);
+    outgoing.set(node.name, []);
+  }
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    for (const dep of node.dependsOn) {
+      if (!byName.has(dep)) continue;
+      incoming.set(node.name, (incoming.get(node.name) ?? 0) + 1);
+      outgoing.get(dep)?.push(node.name);
+    }
+  }
+
+  const groups: CanvasOverviewGroup[] = [];
+  const memberGroup = new Map<string, CanvasOverviewGroup>();
+  const usedIds = new Set(nodes.map((node) => node.name));
+  const allocateGroupId = (base: string): string => {
+    let id = base;
+    let suffix = 2;
+    while (usedIds.has(id)) id = `${base}~${suffix++}`;
+    usedIds.add(id);
+    return id;
+  };
+  const reviewBoundary = new Set<string>();
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    if (node.requiresConfirmation || (node.reviewEdges?.length ?? 0) > 0) {
+      reviewBoundary.add(node.name);
+    }
+    for (const review of node.reviewEdges ?? []) reviewBoundary.add(review.target);
+  }
+  const explicit = new Map<string, CanvasNodeView[]>();
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    if (node.visualGroupId && !reviewBoundary.has(node.name)) {
+      const list = explicit.get(node.visualGroupId) ?? [];
+      list.push(node);
+      explicit.set(node.visualGroupId, list);
+    }
+  }
+  for (const [id, members] of explicit) {
+    const first = members[0];
+    if (!first) continue;
+    const group = makeGroup(
+      allocateGroupId(`visual:${id}`),
+      first.visualGroupLabel ?? id,
+      first.visualGroupKind ?? "stage",
+      members,
+    );
+    groups.push(group);
+    for (const member of members) memberGroup.set(member.name, group);
+  }
+
+  const visited = new Set<string>();
+  const starts = nodes.filter(
+    (node) => !memberGroup.has(node.name) && (incoming.get(node.name) ?? 0) !== 1,
+  );
+  const tryLinear = (start: CanvasNodeView) => {
+    if (visited.has(start.name)) return;
+    const members: CanvasNodeView[] = [];
+    let current: CanvasNodeView | undefined = start;
+    while (
+      current &&
+      !visited.has(current.name) &&
+      !memberGroup.has(current.name) &&
+      !reviewBoundary.has(current.name)
+    ) {
+      members.push(current);
+      visited.add(current.name);
+      const nextNames = outgoing.get(current.name) ?? [];
+      const nextName = nextNames[0];
+      if (!nextName || nextNames.length !== 1 || (incoming.get(nextName) ?? 0) !== 1) break;
+      current = byName.get(nextName);
+    }
+    if (members.length >= 3) {
+      const firstMember = members[0];
+      if (!firstMember) return;
+      const group = makeGroup(
+        allocateGroupId(`linear:${firstMember.name}`),
+        `${firstMember.name} → ${members.at(-1)?.name ?? firstMember.name}`,
+        "stage",
+        members,
+      );
+      groups.push(group);
+      for (const member of members) memberGroup.set(member.name, group);
+    }
+  };
+  for (const node of starts) tryLinear(node);
+  for (const node of nodes)
+    if (!visited.has(node.name) && !memberGroup.has(node.name)) tryLinear(node);
+
+  // 只有收缩后仍是 DAG 才允许显示组。任意组的成员跨过外部节点形成环时，
+  // 保留该组的原节点，避免总览凭空制造循环。
+  const isAcyclic = (collapsed: ReadonlySet<CanvasOverviewGroup>): boolean => {
+    const projected = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
+    const itemOf = (name: string): string => {
+      const group = memberGroup.get(name);
+      return group && collapsed.has(group) ? group.id : name;
+    };
+    for (const node of nodes) {
+      const to = itemOf(node.name);
+      const targets = projected.get(to) ?? new Set<string>();
+      projected.set(to, targets);
+      for (const dep of node.dependsOn) {
+        if (!byName.has(dep)) continue;
+        const from = itemOf(dep);
+        if (from === to) continue;
+        const targets = projected.get(from) ?? new Set<string>();
+        targets.add(to);
+        projected.set(from, targets);
+      }
+    }
+    const visiting = new Set<string>();
+    const done = new Set<string>();
+    const visit = (id: string): boolean => {
+      if (visiting.has(id)) return false;
+      if (done.has(id)) return true;
+      visiting.add(id);
+      for (const next of projected.get(id) ?? []) if (!visit(next)) return false;
+      visiting.delete(id);
+      done.add(id);
+      return true;
+    };
+    return [...projected.keys()].every(visit);
+  };
+  const collapsed = new Set<CanvasOverviewGroup>();
+  for (const group of groups) {
+    const candidate = new Set(collapsed);
+    candidate.add(group);
+    if (isAcyclic(candidate)) collapsed.add(group);
+  }
+
+  // 大图即使被人工确认或审核边界切断、无法合并，也应独立整理总览。
+  // 小图保留冻结位置；详细图始终保留原坐标，不将总览位置写回配方。
+  const hasCollapsedGroup = groups.some(
+    (group) => collapsed.has(group) && !expandedGroups.has(group.id),
+  );
+  const useCompactLayout = hasCollapsedGroup || nodes.length >= COMPACT_OVERVIEW_NODE_COUNT;
+
+  const items: CanvasOverviewItem[] = [];
+  const itemByNode = new Map<string, string>();
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    const group = memberGroup.get(node.name);
+    if (group && collapsed.has(group) && !expandedGroups.has(group.id)) {
+      itemByNode.set(node.name, group.id);
+      continue;
+    }
+    itemByNode.set(node.name, node.name);
+  }
+  const added = new Set<string>();
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    const group = memberGroup.get(node.name);
+    const id =
+      group && collapsed.has(group) && !expandedGroups.has(group.id) ? group.id : node.name;
+    if (added.has(id)) continue;
+    added.add(id);
+    if (group && collapsed.has(group) && !expandedGroups.has(group.id)) {
+      const synthetic: CanvasNodeView = {
+        name: group.id,
+        dept:
+          group.kind === "parallel" ? "并行组" : group.kind === "condition" ? "条件分支" : "阶段",
+        dependsOn: [],
+        requiresConfirmation: group.members.some((member) => member.requiresConfirmation),
+        status:
+          group.tone === "green" ? "delivered" : group.tone === "blue" ? "in_progress" : "no_card",
+      };
+      items.push({ id, view: synthetic, group });
+    } else {
+      if (useCompactLayout) {
+        // 总览使用独立布局；详情页仍保留原节点 position。
+        const { position: _position, ...overviewNode } = node;
+        items.push({ id, view: overviewNode });
+      } else {
+        // 没有压缩组的运行态总览沿用冻结图坐标，避免少量节点被挪到左上角。
+        items.push({ id, view: node });
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  const edgeSet = new Set<string>();
+  const edges: CanvasLayoutEdge[] = [];
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    for (const dep of node.dependsOn) {
+      const from = itemByNode.get(dep);
+      const to = itemByNode.get(node.name);
+      if (!from || !to || from === to) continue;
+      const key = `dependency:${from}->${to}`;
+      if (edgeSet.has(key)) continue;
+      edgeSet.add(key);
+      edges.push({ from, to, back: false });
+    }
+  }
+  // 审核退回是独立的控制边。总览中保留它，避免收起并行组或连续阶段后
+  // “为什么回到上游”变成一条看不懂的普通连线；详细图仍会显示完整标签。
+  for (const node of nodes) {
+    for (const review of node.reviewEdges ?? []) {
+      const from = itemByNode.get(node.name);
+      const to = itemByNode.get(review.target);
+      if (!from || !to || from === to) continue;
+      const key = `review:${from}->${to}`;
+      if (edgeSet.has(key)) continue;
+      edgeSet.add(key);
+      edges.push({ from, to, back: true });
+    }
+  }
+  return { items, edges, groups };
 }

+/** 单侧内容：没有就是「无」，不拿另一侧顶上，也不编造。 */
+function formatToolSide(value: unknown): string {
+  if (value === undefined || value === null) return "—";
+  return typeof value === "string" ? value : stringifyToolValue(value);
+}
+
+/** 中文状态：已失败不显示「完成」，未知状态原样显示。 */
+function statusLabel(status: TimelineToolCall["status"], t: (key: string) => string): string {
+  if (status === "running") return t("Running");
+  if (status === "success") return t("Done");
+  if (status === "error") return t("Failed");
+  return status;
+}
+
+/** 可读耗时：一头一尾都有真实时刻才算，缺任一头＝不显示，不猜。 */
+function toolDurationText(item: TimelineToolCall, t: (key: string) => string): string | undefined {
+  if (!item.finishedAt) return undefined;
+  const started = Date.parse(item.createdAt);
+  const finished = Date.parse(item.finishedAt);
+  if (!Number.isFinite(started) || !Number.isFinite(finished) || finished < started) {
+    return undefined;
+  }
+  return t("Took {duration}").replace("{duration}", formatWorkedDuration(finished - started));
+}
+
+/** 原始时间与技术名留在悬停标题里，随时可查。 */
+function toolTimeTitle(item: TimelineToolCall): string {
+  return item.finishedAt ? `${item.createdAt} → ${item.finishedAt}` : item.createdAt;
 }

+/** 可读耗时（中文单位）：秒/分/时，最小 1 秒。 */
 function formatWorkedDuration(durationMs: number): string {
   const totalSeconds = Math.max(1, Math.round(durationMs / 1000));
   if (totalSeconds < 60) {
-    return `${totalSeconds}s`;
+    return `${totalSeconds} 秒`;
   }
   const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
   const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
   if (minutes < 60) {
-    return seconds > 0 ? `${minutes}m ${seconds}s` : `${minutes}m`;
+    return seconds > 0 ? `${minutes} 分 ${seconds} 秒` : `${minutes} 分`;
   }
   const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
   const remMinutes = minutes % 60;
-  return remMinutes > 0 ? `${hours}h ${remMinutes}m` : `${hours}h`;
+  return remMinutes > 0 ? `${hours} 小时 ${remMinutes} 分` : `${hours} 小时`;
 }

+/** 首跑清单是当前快照：刷新原位置的内容，历史原件与其他扩展消息不变。 */
+export function refreshFirstRunChecklist(
+  transcript: readonly TranscriptMessage[],
+): readonly TranscriptMessage[] {
+  const firstIndex = transcript.findIndex(
+    (item) => item.kind === "custom" && item.customType === "pao-firstrun",
+  );
+  if (firstIndex < 0) return transcript;
+  const first = transcript[firstIndex]!;
+  let latest = first;
+  const result = transcript.filter((item, index) => {
+    if (item.kind !== "custom" || item.customType !== "pao-firstrun") return true;
+    latest = item;
+    return index === firstIndex;
+  });
+  if (latest === first) return transcript;
+  result[firstIndex] = { ...latest, id: first.id };
+  return result;
+}
+
 function isUserMessage(item: TimelineTranscriptItem | undefined): boolean {
   return item?.kind === "message" && item.role === "user";
 }

+/** 当前保存的工作台宽度；没有偏好时使用界面默认 360px。 */
+export function workbenchWidthPixels(style: CSSProperties): number {

-function workbenchBounds(_panel: HTMLElement, main: HTMLElement): PaneWidthBounds {

+/**
+ * 工作台上限按 `.main` 容器的 55% 算，与 task-status 布局的工具列约束一致。
+ *
+ * 窄窗手动三列时工具列由 CSS 的 33% 分配，面板实际宽度是唯一真值；手柄读取当前盒宽并
+ * 固定在该值，保持不能拖，同时不让键盘或拖拽把偏好写成看不见的宽度。
+ */
+function workbenchBounds(panel: HTMLElement, container: HTMLElement): PaneWidthBounds {
+  if (window.innerWidth <= NARROW_VIEWPORT_WIDTH) {
+    const width = Math.round(panel.getBoundingClientRect().width);
+    return { min: width, max: width };
+  }
+  const layout = container.closest(".main") ?? container;
+  const max = Math.floor(Math.min(1200, layout.clientWidth * 0.55));
   return { min: 320, max };
 }

-function DetailField({ label, value }: { readonly label: string; readonly value: string }) {
+/** 预算分项：单位与内核 `CardBudget` 契约一致（turns/toolCalls/cost/collab_quota），未配置的项不显示、不填假 0。 */
+function cardBudgetFields(
+  budget: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined,
+  t: (key: string) => string,
+): readonly { readonly label: string; readonly value: string }[] {
+  if (!budget) return [];
+  const fields: { label: string; value: string }[] = [];
+  const push = (key: string, label: string) => {
+    const raw = budget[key];
+    if (raw === undefined || raw === null) return;
+    fields.push({ label, value: String(raw) });
+  };
+  push("turns", t("Budget turns"));
+  push("toolCalls", t("Budget tool calls"));
+  push("cost", t("Budget cost"));
+  push("collab_quota", t("Budget collaboration quota"));
+  return fields;
+}
+
+/** 预算里不认识的键：不丢，收进可展开的原始值，不默认铺在普通详情里。 */
+function cardBudgetUnknownKeys(
+  budget: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined,
+): Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined {
+  if (!budget) return undefined;
+  const known = new Set(["turns", "toolCalls", "cost", "collab_quota"]);
+  const rest = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(budget).filter(([key]) => !known.has(key)));
+  return Object.keys(rest).length > 0 ? rest : undefined;
+}
+
+function DetailField({
+  label,
+  value,
+  title,
+}: {
+  readonly label: string;
+  readonly value: string;
+  /** 原始值（如 UTC ISO 时刻）挂在 title 上：给人看本地时间，原始值仍可查看。 */
+  readonly title?: string;
+}) {
   return (
     <div className="kanban-detail__field">
       <dt>{label}</dt>
-      <dd>{value}</dd>
+      <dd title={title}>{value}</dd>
     </div>
   );
 }

diff --git a/apps/desktop/src/lib/string-utils.ts b/apps/desktop/src/lib/string-utils.ts
index 7300d59e..f345f849 100644
--- a/apps/desktop/src/lib/string-utils.ts
+++ b/apps/desktop/src/lib/string-utils.ts
@@ -31,3 +31,18 @@ export function formatRelativeTime(value: string): string {
   const diffYears = Math.floor(diffDays / 365);
   return `${diffYears}y`;
 }
+
+/**
+ * UTC ISO（或任意可解析时刻）→ 本地「YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm」。
+ * 与内核 `src/pao/shared/time.ts` 的 `utcToLocalDisplay` 同一口径；渲染层不能深引内核，
+ * 所以在这里保留一份等价实现。坏值/空值原样返回（展示面不抛）。
+ */
+export function formatLocalMinute(value: string): string {
+  const timestamp = Date.parse(value);
+  if (Number.isNaN(timestamp)) {
+    return value;
+  }
+  const date = new Date(timestamp);
+  const pad = (n: number): string => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
+  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())} ${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
+}

+test("first-run checklist refreshes one row across live updates, session switches and relaunch", async ({}, testInfo) => {
+  test.setTimeout(120_000);
+  const userDataDir = await makeUserDataDir();
+  const agentDir = join(userDataDir, "agent");
+  const workspacePath = await makeWorkspace("first-run-refresh");
+  await seedAgentDir(agentDir);
+  // 真扩展、真会话日志；清单内容由夹具生成，不请求外部模型。
+  await writeProjectExtension(
+    workspacePath,
+    "first-run.ts",
+    String.raw`
+export default function firstRunFixture(pi) {
+  let refresh = 0;
+  const send = () => pi.sendMessage({ customType: "pao-firstrun", content: "── 首跑清单（§16.1）──\n今日待办：" + refresh + "\n第二行：保留换行", display: true });
+  pi.on("session_start", send);
+  pi.registerCommand("refresh-checklist", { description: "Refresh fixture checklist", handler: async () => {
+    refresh += 1;
+    send();
+    pi.sendMessage({ customType: "note", content: "普通记录 " + refresh, display: true });
+  }});
+}
+`,
+  );
+  const firstRun = await launchDesktop(userDataDir, {
+    agentDir,
+    initialWorkspaces: [workspacePath],
+    scrubProviderEnv: true,
+  });
+  try {
+    const window = await firstRun.firstWindow();
+    await waitForWorkspaceByPath(window, workspacePath);
+    await createNamedThread(window, "Checklist A");
+    // 新建空会话按需启动内核；第一次命令触发 session_start 与随后的清单刷新。
+    await window.getByTestId("composer").fill("/refresh-checklist ");
+    await window.getByTestId("composer").press("Enter");
+    const row = window.getByTestId("timeline-first-run");
+    await expect(row).toHaveCount(1);
+    await expect(row).toContainText("今日待办：1");
+    const rowId = await row
+      .locator("xpath=ancestor::*[@data-message-id][1]")
+      .getAttribute("data-message-id");
+    for (const value of [2, 3]) {
+      await window.getByTestId("composer").fill("/refresh-checklist ");
+      await window.getByTestId("composer").press("Enter");
+      await expect(row).toHaveCount(1);
+      await expect(row).toContainText("今日待办：" + value);
+    }
+    expect(
+      await row.locator("xpath=ancestor::*[@data-message-id][1]").getAttribute("data-message-id"),
+    ).toBe(rowId);
+    await expect(customRows(window)).toHaveCount(3);
+    expect(
+      await row
+        .locator(".timeline-item__checklist")
+        .evaluate((element) => getComputedStyle(element).whiteSpace),
+    ).toBe("pre-wrap");
+    // 原始日志保留三次快照，显示层只保留最新一条。
+    const transcript = await getSelectedTranscript(window);
+    expect(
+      transcript?.transcript.filter(
+        (item) => item.kind === "custom" && item.customType === "pao-firstrun",
+      ),
+    ).toHaveLength(3);
+    await createNamedThread(window, "Checklist B");
+    await window.getByTestId("composer").fill("/refresh-checklist ");
+    await window.getByTestId("composer").press("Enter");
+    await expect(row).toContainText("今日待办：1");
+    await selectSession(window, "Checklist A");
+    await expect(row).toHaveCount(1);
+    await expect(row).toContainText("今日待办：3");
+    await firstRun.electronApp.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => {
+      const win = BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!;
+      if (win.isMinimized()) win.restore();
+      win.showInactive();
+    });
+    await window.screenshot({
+      path: testInfo.outputPath("checklist-and-sidebar.png"),
+      animations: "disabled",
+    });
+  } finally {
+    await firstRun.close();
+  }
+  const secondRun = await launchDesktop(userDataDir, { agentDir, scrubProviderEnv: true });
+  try {
+    const window = await secondRun.firstWindow();
+    await selectSession(window, "Checklist A");
+    await expect(window.getByTestId("timeline-first-run")).toHaveCount(1);
+    // 重开会话触发 session_start，新快照为 0，并覆盖上次运行的 3。
+    await expect(window.getByTestId("timeline-first-run")).toContainText("今日待办：0");
+    // 恢复出的历史先显示，扩展命令随后就绪；等命令注册后才提交。
+    await expect
+      .poll(async () => {
+        const state = await getDesktopState(window);
+        const key = `${state.selectedWorkspaceId}:${state.selectedSessionId}`;
+        return (
+          state.sessionCommandsBySession[key]?.some(
+            (command) => command.name === "refresh-checklist",
+          ) ?? false
+        );
+      })
+      .toBe(true);
+    await window.getByTestId("composer").fill("/refresh-checklist ");
+    await window.getByTestId("composer").press("Enter");
+    await expect(window.getByTestId("timeline-first-run")).toHaveCount(1);
+    await expect(window.getByTestId("timeline-first-run")).toContainText("今日待办：1");
+    await expect(customRows(window)).toHaveCount(4);
+  } finally {
+    await secondRun.close();
+  }
+});

+/**
+ * 界面按设计把家目录下的路径缩写成 `~/…`（`app-store.ts` 的 `withHomeAsTilde`，与 HEAD 相同）。
+ * Windows 的测试临时目录就在家目录下，所以这里按同一条规则算期望值；
+ * Linux/macOS 的临时目录在 `/tmp`，规则不触发，期望值仍是原路径。
+ */
+function asSurfacePath(path: string): string {
+  const home = homedir();
+  return path.startsWith(`${home}${sep}`) ? `~${path.slice(home.length)}` : path;
+}

+async function openSecondarySurface(
+  window: Page,
+  view: "Settings" | "Skills" | "Extensions",
+): Promise<void> {
+  if (view === "Settings") {
+    await window.keyboard.press(desktopShortcut(","));
+    return;
+  }
+  await window.keyboard.press(desktopShortcut("K"));
+  const input = window.getByTestId("command-palette-input");
+  await expect(input).toBeVisible();
+  await input.fill(view);
+  await window.getByRole("option", { name: new RegExp(`^${view}`) }).click();
+}

 const proofDir = process.env.PI_APP_THREAD_MENU_PROOF_DIR;
 
+test("右键删除对话：取消保留消息，确认删除文件与目录项，其他对话保留且重开不复现", async () => {
+  const userDataDir = await makeUserDataDir();
+  const workspacePath = await makeWorkspace("delete-thread-workspace");
+  const app = await launchDesktop(userDataDir, { initialWorkspaces: [workspacePath] });
+  let target: { workspaceId: string; sessionId: string };
+  let sessionFile: string;
+  try {
+    const page = await app.firstWindow();
+    await createNamedThread(page, "待删除对话");
+    const state = await getDesktopState(page);
+    target = { workspaceId: state.selectedWorkspaceId, sessionId: state.selectedSessionId };
+    sessionFile = await sessionFilePathFromCatalog(userDataDir, target);
+    await createNamedThread(page, "保留对话");
+    const kept = (await getDesktopState(page)).selectedSessionId;
+    const row = page.locator(`.session-row[data-session-id="${target.sessionId}"]`);
+    await row.click({ button: "right" });
+    await captureProof(page, "删除对话右键菜单.png");
+    const cancelDelete = page.waitForEvent("dialog").then(async (dialog) => {
+      expect(dialog.message()).toContain("待删除对话");
+      await dialog.dismiss();
+    });
+    await Promise.all([
+      cancelDelete,
+      row.getByRole("menu").locator('[data-thread-action="delete-thread"]').click(),
+    ]);
+    await expect(row).toHaveCount(1);
+    await access(sessionFile);
+    await row.click({ button: "right" });
+    const confirmDelete = page.waitForEvent("dialog").then((dialog) => dialog.accept());
+    await Promise.all([
+      confirmDelete,
+      row.getByRole("menu").locator('[data-thread-action="delete-thread"]').click(),
+    ]);
+    await expect(row).toHaveCount(0);
+    await expect.poll(async () => (await getDesktopState(page)).selectedSessionId).toBe(kept);
+    await expect
+      .poll(async () => {
+        try {
+          await access(sessionFile);
+          return "exists";
+        } catch (error) {
+          if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
+          return "deleted";
+        }
+      })
+      .toBe("deleted");
+    await page.getByTestId("thread-header-menu").click();
+    await expect(
+      page.locator('.chat-header__menu [data-thread-action="delete-thread"]'),
+    ).toBeVisible();
+    await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
+  } finally {
+    await app.close();
+  }
+  const reopened = await launchDesktop(userDataDir);
+  try {
+    const page = await reopened.firstWindow();
+    await expect(page.locator(`.session-row[data-session-id="${target!.sessionId}"]`)).toHaveCount(
+      0,
+    );
+    await expect(page.locator(".session-row", { hasText: "保留对话" })).toHaveCount(1);
+    // 删除当前选中对话时回落到其他对话，不能继续指向已删除的会话。
+    await createNamedThread(page, "删除当前对话");
+    const selected = (await getDesktopState(page)).selectedSessionId;
+    const row = page.locator(`.session-row[data-session-id="${selected}"]`);
+    await row.click({ button: "right" });
+    const confirmDelete = page.waitForEvent("dialog").then((dialog) => dialog.accept());
+    await Promise.all([
+      confirmDelete,
+      row.getByRole("menu").locator('[data-thread-action="delete-thread"]').click(),
+    ]);
+    await expect(row).toHaveCount(0);
+    await expect
+      .poll(async () => (await getDesktopState(page)).selectedSessionId)
+      .not.toBe(selected);
+    await expect(page.locator(".session-row", { hasText: "保留对话" })).toHaveCount(1);
+  } finally {
+    await reopened.close();
+  }
+});

+test("hiding the workbench keeps the mounted terminal and its output until reopened", async () => {
+  test.setTimeout(60_000);
+  const fixture = await prepareWorkspace();
+  const harness = await launchDesktop(fixture.userDataDir, {
+    agentDir: fixture.agentDir,
+    initialWorkspaces: [fixture.workspacePath],
+    testMode: "background",
+    // PowerShell 的 PSReadLine 会把快速键入的大写序列转成编辑命令，
+    // 使用系统 cmd.exe 才能直接验证“键入、隐藏、恢复后输出仍在”。
+    envOverrides: { SHELL: process.env.ComSpec },
+  });
+  try {
+    const window = await harness.firstWindow();
+    await selectSession(window, TASK_A);
+    await addTool(window, "Terminal");
+    const terminal = window.getByTestId("integrated-terminal");
+    await terminal.locator(".xterm").click();
+    await window.keyboard.type(shellCommands([setEnvCommand("PI_GUI_MOUNTED_CANARY", "retained")]));
+    await window.keyboard.press("Enter");
+    await window.keyboard.type(echoEnvCommand("MOUNTED_", "PI_GUI_MOUNTED_CANARY"));
+    await window.keyboard.press("Enter");
+    await expect(terminal.locator(".xterm-rows")).toContainText("MOUNTED_retained");
+    const host = window.locator(".task-status-workbench-host");
+    await expect(host).toBeVisible();
+    await toggleWorkbench(window);
+    await expect(host).toBeHidden();
+    await expect(terminal).toHaveCount(1);
+    await expect(terminal.locator(".xterm-rows")).toContainText("MOUNTED_retained");
+    await toggleWorkbench(window);
+    await expect(host).toBeVisible();
+    await expect(terminal).toHaveCount(1);
+    await expect(terminal.locator(".xterm-rows")).toContainText("MOUNTED_retained");
+  } finally {
+    await harness.close();
+  }
+});

+test("wide workbench width controls announce and apply the CSS maximum", async () => {
+  test.setTimeout(60_000);
+  const fixture = await prepareWorkspace();
+  const harness = await launchDesktop(fixture.userDataDir, {
+    agentDir: fixture.agentDir,
+    initialWorkspaces: [fixture.workspacePath],
+    testMode: "background",
+  });
+  try {
+    await harness.electronApp.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => {
+      BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]?.setContentSize(1440, 900);
+    });
+    const window = await harness.firstWindow();
+    await selectSession(window, TASK_A);
+    await openWorkbench(window);
+    const panelWidth = async () =>
+      Math.round((await window.getByTestId("workbench").boundingBox())!.width);
+    await expect.poll(panelWidth).toBe(360);
+    const mainWidth = await window.locator(".main").evaluate((element) => element.clientWidth);
+    const expectedMax = Math.floor(mainWidth * 0.55);
+    expect(expectedMax).toBeLessThan(Math.floor(mainWidth * 0.65));
+    const handle = window.getByRole("separator", { name: "Side panel width" });
+    await expect(handle).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuemax", String(expectedMax));
+
+    await handle.focus();
+    await handle.press("End");
+    await expect.poll(panelWidth).toBe(expectedMax);
+    await expect(handle).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(expectedMax));
+
+    await handle.press("Home");
+    await expect.poll(panelWidth).toBe(320);
+    const start = (await handle.boundingBox())!;
+    await window.mouse.move(start.x + 3, start.y + 160);
+    await window.mouse.down();
+    await window.mouse.move(start.x - mainWidth, start.y + 160, { steps: 10 });
+    await window.mouse.up();
+    await expect.poll(panelWidth).toBe(expectedMax);
+    await expect(handle).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(expectedMax));
+  } finally {
+    await harness.close();
+  }
+});
+
+test("compact status keeps the work surface full width beside open tools", async () => {
+  test.setTimeout(60_000);
+  const fixture = await prepareWorkspace();
+  const harness = await launchDesktop(fixture.userDataDir, {
+    agentDir: fixture.agentDir,
+    initialWorkspaces: [fixture.workspacePath],
+    testMode: "background",
+  });
+  try {
+    await harness.electronApp.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => {
+      BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]?.setContentSize(1440, 900);
+    });
+    const window = await harness.firstWindow();
+    await selectSession(window, TASK_A);
+    await openWorkbench(window);
+    await expect(window.getByTestId("task-status")).toBeVisible();
+    await window.getByTestId("task-status-collapse").click();
+
+    const shortbar = window.getByTestId("task-status-expand");
+    const tools = window.locator(".task-status-workbench-host");
+    const main = window.locator(".main");
+    await expect(shortbar).toBeVisible();
+    await expect(tools).toBeVisible();
+    await expect
+      .poll(() =>
+        main.evaluate((element) => getComputedStyle(element).gridTemplateColumns.split(" ").length),
+      )
+      .toBe(2);
+    const shortbarBox = (await shortbar.boundingBox())!;
+    const toolsBox = (await tools.boundingBox())!;
+    expect(shortbarBox.x + shortbarBox.width).toBeLessThanOrEqual(toolsBox.x + 1);
+    expect(shortbarBox.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual((await main.boundingBox())!.x + 8);
+    const transcriptBox = (await window.getByTestId("timeline-pane").boundingBox())!;
+    expect(transcriptBox.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(shortbarBox.y + shortbarBox.height);
+
+    const composer = (await window.locator(".composer").boundingBox())!;
+    const laneGap = await main.evaluate((element) =>
+      getComputedStyle(element).columnGap === "normal"
+        ? 0
+        : Number.parseFloat(getComputedStyle(element).columnGap),
+    );
+    expect(composer.x + composer.width).toBeCloseTo(toolsBox.x - laneGap, 0);
+
+    await toggleWorkbench(window);
+    await expect(tools).toBeHidden();
+    await expect(shortbar).toBeVisible();
+    const closedShortbar = (await shortbar.boundingBox())!;
+    const closedTranscript = (await window.getByTestId("timeline-pane").boundingBox())!;
+    expect(closedTranscript.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(closedShortbar.y + closedShortbar.height);
+  } finally {
+    await harness.close();
+  }
+});

+test("saved wide tool width uses its real CSS width when deciding overview space", async () => {
+  test.setTimeout(90_000);
+  const fixture = await prepareWorkspace();
+  const options = {
+    agentDir: fixture.agentDir,
+    initialWorkspaces: [fixture.workspacePath],
+    testMode: "background" as const,
+  };
+  let harness = await launchDesktop(fixture.userDataDir, options);
+  const setWindowWidth = async (width: number) => {
+    const window = await harness.firstWindow();
+    await window.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
+    await expect.poll(() => window.evaluate(() => globalThis.innerWidth)).toBe(width);
+  };
+  try {
+    let window = await harness.firstWindow();
+    await window.setViewportSize({ width: 2600, height: 900 });
+    await expect.poll(() => window.evaluate(() => globalThis.innerWidth)).toBe(2600);
+    await selectSession(window, TASK_A);
+    await openWorkbench(window);
+    const handle = window.getByRole("separator", { name: "Side panel width" });
+    const enoughSpace = await window.locator(".main").evaluate((main) => ({
+      mainWidth: main.clientWidth,
+      handleMax: Math.min(1200, Math.floor(main.clientWidth * 0.55)),
+    }));
+    expect(enoughSpace.handleMax).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1200);
+    await expect(handle).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuemax", String(enoughSpace.handleMax));
+    await handle.focus();
+    await handle.press("End");
+    await expect
+      .poll(() => window.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("pi-gui.workbench-width")))
+      .toBe("1200");
+
+    await harness.close();
+    harness = await launchDesktop(fixture.userDataDir, options);
+    window = await harness.firstWindow();
+    await selectSession(window, TASK_A);
+    await openWorkbench(window);
+    await setWindowWidth(1900);
+    await expect(window.getByTestId("task-status")).toBeVisible();
+    await expect.poll(() => window.getByTestId("task-status").isVisible()).toBe(true);
+    await expect(window.getByTestId("task-status-expand")).toHaveCount(0);
+    const remaining = await window.evaluate(() => {
+      const main = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".main")!;
+      const tools = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".task-status-workbench-host")!;
+      return main.clientWidth - tools.clientWidth;
+    });
+    expect(remaining).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(704);
+
+    await setWindowWidth(1400);
+    await expect.poll(() => window.getByTestId("task-status-expand").isVisible()).toBe(true);
+    await setWindowWidth(1900);
+    await expect(window.getByTestId("task-status")).toBeVisible();
+    expect(await window.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("pi-gui.workbench-width"))).toBe(
+      "1200",
+    );
+  } finally {
+    await harness.close();
+  }
+});

+/**
+ * 收起侧栏。

+ * 侧栏收起后 `[data-testid="sidebar-toggle"]` 本身会被卸载（`App.tsx` 在 collapsed 时改渲染顶栏按钮），
+ * 快捷键 `Ctrl+B` 才是全局的收起/展开入口；`el-probe.cjs` 的实测同样显示

+    await toggle.click();
+    return;
+  }
+  await window.keyboard.press(desktopShortcut("B"));

+/**
+ * CSS 属性值里反斜杠是转义符，Windows 的 worktree 身份就是绝对路径，
+ * 直接插进去会把 `\U`、`\A` 这类序列吃掉反斜杠，选择器永远匹配不到。这里按 CSS 规则转义。
+ */
+function cssAttributeValue(value: string): string {
+  return value.replace(/\\/g, "\\\\").replace(/"/g, '\\"');
+}

 export async function reloadDesktopRenderer(window: Page): Promise<void> {
-  await window.reload();
+  // 主进程会拦下页面重载，先把渲染层还在防抖窗口里的草稿交给宿主再真正 reload
+  // （main.ts 的 did-start-navigation 处理）。首个 reload 因此可能以 ERR_ABORTED 结束：
+  // 那时导航已被取消、真正的重载还没发起，导航会被重新挂起，所以重试是安全的——
+  // 中止是这条拦截路径的正常表现，不是断言失败。组合跑时拦下的导航偶尔连续中止两次，
+  // 所以在同一条件下限次重试，仍不放过其它错误。
+  const attempts = 3;
+  for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt += 1) {
+    try {
+      await window.reload();
+      break;
+    } catch (error: unknown) {
+      const aborted =
+        error instanceof Error && /ERR_ABORTED|frame was detached/i.test(error.message);
+      if (!aborted || attempt >= attempts) throw error;
+    }
+  }
   await window.waitForLoadState("domcontentloaded");
   await window.waitForFunction(() => Boolean(globalThis.window.piApp), undefined, {
     timeout: 15_000,

+      name: "已确认",
+      dept: "",
+      dependsOn: [],
+      requiresConfirmation: true,
+      status: "delivered",
+      confirmation: "2026-10-11T12:00:00Z",
+    },
+  ];

+  ]);

+  expect(desktopPathKey("/项目/Card")).not.toBe(desktopPathKey("/项目/card"));

+test("折行总览排序大图及收起后的单条主干，保留审核回边", () => {
+  const nodes = Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, index) => ({
+    name: `步骤${index}`,
+    dept: "工程",
+    dependsOn: index === 0 ? [] : [`步骤${index - 1}`],
+    requiresConfirmation: index % 4 === 3,
+    ...(index === 15
+      ? { reviewEdges: [{ target: "步骤3", limit: 2, overflowAction: "停滞" as const }] }
+      : {}),
+  }));
+  const graph = buildCanvasOverview(nodes);
+  const order = overviewChainOrder({ ...graph, items: [...graph.items].reverse() });
+  expect(order).toHaveLength(graph.items.length);
+  expect(order?.[0]).toBe(graph.items[0]?.id);
+  expect(order?.at(-1)).toBe("步骤15");
+  expect(graph.edges.find((edge) => edge.back)).toEqual({
+    from: "步骤15",
+    to: "步骤3",
+    back: true,
+  });
+  expect(
+    overviewChainOrder(buildCanvasOverview(nodes, new Set(graph.groups.map((group) => group.id)))),
+  ).toEqual(nodes.map((node) => node.name));
+});

   cwd: string;
   signal: AbortSignal;
   env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
-  /** Executable name or path; tests pass a fake. */
+  /** Executable name or path; tests may pass a fake. Takes precedence over {@link GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV}. */
   gh?: string;
 }
 
+/**
+ * 让调用方指定「怎么启动 gh」的环境变量，值是 JSON 字符串数组：第一项是程序，其余是前置参数。
+ *
+ * 为什么需要它：在 Windows 上没有任何办法靠往 PATH 里放一个文件来冒充 `gh`——`execFile` 走
+ * `CreateProcess` 且不经 shell，无扩展名脚本会被按 `PATHEXT` 跳过（于是跑到真机上的 gh.exe），
+ * `.cmd`/`.bat` 被 libuv 直接拒成 `spawn EINVAL`。所以测试要交假 gh，只能由调用方显式告诉
+ * 产品「执行哪个程序、带什么前置参数」。
+ *
+ * 不设这个变量时，`execFile` 仍然执行 `"gh"`，默认行为与原来完全一致。
+ */
+export const GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV = "GITHUB_GH_EXECUTABLE";
+
+/** 要执行的 gh 命令：程序加上前置参数（前面会被塞进 gh 的固定参数之前）。 */
+export interface GhCommand {
+  readonly program: string;
+  readonly prefix: readonly string[];
+}
+
+/**
+ * 解析 {@link GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV}。值必须是非空的 JSON 字符串数组，例如
+ * `["node", "--", "fake-gh.cjs"]`；不设或为空时返回 `{ program: "gh", prefix: [] }`。
+ *
+ * 配置写错时直接抛错而不静默退回真 gh：静默退回会让「假 gh 没生效」表现成「打到了真网络」，
+ * 更难排查。
+ */
+export function ghCommand(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): GhCommand {
+  const raw = env[GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV]?.trim();
+  if (!raw) return { program: "gh", prefix: [] };
+  if (!raw.startsWith("["))
+    throw new Error(
+      `${GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV} 须为 JSON 字符串数组，例如 ["node", "--", "fake-gh.cjs"]。`,
+    );
+  let parsed: unknown;
+  try {
+    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
+  } catch (error) {
+    throw new Error(`${GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV} 不是合法 JSON：${String(error).slice(0, 200)}`);
+  }
+  if (
+    !Array.isArray(parsed) ||
+    parsed.length === 0 ||
+    parsed.some((item) => typeof item !== "string" || item.length === 0)
+  )
+    throw new Error(`${GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV} 须为非空的字符串数组。`);
+  const [program, ...prefix] = parsed as string[];
+  return { program, prefix };
+}
+

+/**
+ * 通过环境变量把假 gh 交给产品（见 source.ts 的 GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV）。
+ *
+ * 不能像以前那样把 `fake-gh.mjs` 的路径直接当 `options.gh` 传进去：那是一条在 Windows 上
+ * 执行不了的路径——`.mjs` 不是 PE，`execFile` 会报 `spawn EFTYPE`。这里改成「node + 前置参数」，
+ * 由 `node` 去跑替身，平台无关。
+ *
+ * 跑一个临时 `.cjs` 启动器而不是直接 `["node", "fake-gh.mjs"]`，是为了固定住「程序 + 前置参数」
+ * 这种形态：启动器用动态 `import()` 加载 ESM 替身（`require()` 不接受 `file://` URL）。
+ */
+async function fakeGhCommand(fake: string, env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): Promise<string> {
+  const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "pi-gui-gh-cmd-"));
+  const launcher = join(directory, "fake-gh.cjs");
+  await writeFile(
+    launcher,
+    [
+      '"use strict";',
+      `import(${JSON.stringify(pathToFileURL(fake).href)}).catch((error) => {`,
+      "  process.stderr.write(String(error?.stack ?? error));",
+      "  process.exitCode = 1;",
+      "});",
+      "",
+    ].join("\n"),
+  );
+  const command = JSON.stringify([process.execPath, "--", launcher]);
+  env[GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV] = command;
+  return command;
+}

+await test("ghCommand: unset stays on the system gh, explicit options.gh wins", () => {
+  // 不设环境变量时必须是原来的行为：执行名为 gh 的程序、不带任何前置参数。

+  assert.deepEqual(ghCommand({ [GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV]: "   " }), { program: "gh", prefix: [] });
+  // 数组形态：第一项是程序，其余是前置参数。
+  assert.deepEqual(ghCommand({ [GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV]: '["node","--","fake-gh.cjs"]' }), {
+    program: "node",
+    prefix: ["--", "fake-gh.cjs"],
+  });
+  // 配置写错就直接报错，不静默退回真 gh。
+  assert.throws(() => ghCommand({ [GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV]: "node -- fake.cjs" }), /JSON 字符串数组/);
+  assert.throws(() => ghCommand({ [GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV]: "[not json" }), /不是合法 JSON/);
+  assert.throws(() => ghCommand({ [GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV]: "[]" }), /非空的字符串数组/);
+  assert.throws(() => ghCommand({ [GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV]: '["node", 7]' }), /非空的字符串数组/);

@@ -6,6 +6,47 @@ import type { ReviewTarget } from "./contract";
 const execute = promisify(execFile);
 const SHA = /^[a-f0-9]{40,64}$/;
 
+/**
+ * 可选的 `gh` 启动配置（环境变量）。取值只有两种形态：
+ * - 不设置：按 PATH 找 `gh`（默认行为与兼容契约，完全不变）。
+ * - JSON 字符串数组：`["program", "前置参数", ...]`。`gh` 自己的参数追加在数组之后。
+ *   例：`["C:\\Program Files\\nodejs\\node.exe", "--", "C:\\tmp\\fake-gh.cjs"]`。
+ *   用数组而不是空白分隔的一行，是因为 Windows 的程序路径普遍带空格
+ *   （`C:\Program Files\...`），按空白切分必然切坏。
+ * 存在的理由：Windows 上没有任何办法靠「往 PATH 里放个文件」冒充 `gh`——无扩展名脚本被
+ * CreateProcess 跳过，`.cmd/.bat` 被 node 的 execFile 拒成 `spawn EINVAL`。测试需要把
+ * 「本平台真能启动的进程 + 它的前置参数」直接交给产品，而不是靠 PATH 和扩展名去猜。
+ */
+export const GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV = "PR_REVIEW_GH_EXECUTABLE";
+
+export interface GhCommand {
+  readonly program: string;
+  readonly prefix: readonly string[];
+}
+
+export function ghCommand(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): GhCommand {
+  const raw = env[GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV]?.trim();
+  if (!raw) return { program: "gh", prefix: [] };
+  if (!raw.startsWith("["))
+    throw new Error(
+      `${GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV} 须为 JSON 字符串数组，例如 ["node", "--", "fake-gh.cjs"]。`,
+    );
+  let parsed: unknown;
+  try {
+    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
+  } catch (error) {
+    throw new Error(`${GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV} 不是合法 JSON：${String(error).slice(0, 200)}`);
+  }
+  if (
+    !Array.isArray(parsed) ||
+    parsed.length === 0 ||
+    parsed.some((item) => typeof item !== "string" || item.length === 0)
+  )
+    throw new Error(`${GH_EXECUTABLE_ENV} 须为非空的字符串数组。`);
+  const [program, ...prefix] = parsed as string[];
+  return { program, prefix };
+}
+
 function text(value: unknown, name: string): string {
   if (typeof value !== "string" || !value || value.length > 4096)
     throw new Error(`GitHub returned an invalid ${name}.`);
@@ -29,10 +70,15 @@ export class ReviewRepository {

         timeout: 20_000,
@@ -53,13 +99,14 @@ export class ReviewRepository {

   DesktopSessionIncrement,

+/**
+ * 部门目录与详情（E2b）：类型镜像自 PAO 的 `src/pao/desktop/dept.ts`。
+ *
+ * 为什么不在 `@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/pao/desktop` 直接 import type：该入口目前
+ * 没有桶导出那份文件，跨包拿不到；等内核补齐之前先镜像，字段名与形状逐字对齐，
+ * 好处是这份契约能在浏览器端编译（不依赖内核类型），坏处是内核改字段要同步。
+ * 渲染层只用本文件声明的类型，不 import 内核模块——保持扩展与内核的窄边界。
+ */

+}
+export interface PaoDeptCatalogRow {

+/** 复用同一附件前缀解析，只提取文件路径供辅助对话检查当前可读性。 */
+export function collectFileAttachmentPaths(
+  messages: readonly unknown[],
+): readonly string[] {
+  const paths = messages.flatMap((message) =>
+    isRecord(message)
+      ? messageAttachments(message)
+          .filter(
+            (attachment): attachment is Extract<
+              SessionTranscriptAttachment,
+              { readonly kind: "file" }
+            > => attachment.kind === "file",
+          )
+          .map((attachment) => attachment.fsPath)
+      : [],
+  );
+  return [...new Set(paths)];
+}

+  /**
+   * 读取辅助对话需要的主会话材料。
+   *
+   * 已运行的主会话直接读取同一个 SessionManager，保证 leaf、projection 和模型配置来自
+   * 同一时刻。未加载的会话以只读字节读取交给 pi 的公开解析函数，再装入 in-memory
+   * manager；旧 schema 只在内存迁移。这里刻意不调用 ensureRecord，避免仅为了辅助对话
+   * 而创建、恢复或改变主控执行状态。
+   */
+  async getSideChatSource(sessionRef: SessionRef): Promise<SideChatSourceSnapshot> {
+    const record = this.records.get(sessionKey(sessionRef));
+    if (record?.session && !record.closed) {
+      const session = record.session;
+      const projection = session.sessionManager.buildSessionProjection();
+      const config = deriveSessionConfig(session.sessionManager) ?? record.config;
+      const blockImages = session.settingsManager.getBlockImages();
+      const limitations: string[] = [];
+      const sourceBusy = isRecordBusy(record);
+      if (sourceBusy) {
+        limitations.push(
+          "主对话仍在运行；已读取当前可用的已完成边界，不包含尚未结束的模型输出或工具结果，也无需等待、暂停或取消主控。",
+        );
+      }
+      if (session.isCompacting) {
+        limitations.push("主对话正在压缩；已读取压缩开始前的一致有效上下文，压缩完成后可再次同步。");
+      }
+      if (record.status === "failed") {
+        limitations.push("主对话最近一次运行失败；现有 projection 仅代表已持久化的有效材料。");
+      }
+      return {
+        ref: { ...record.ref },
+        workspacePath: record.workspace.path,
+        status: record.status,
+        boundaryId: session.sessionManager.getLeafId(),
+        capturedAt: nowIso(),
+        sourceBusy,
+        messages: projection.messages,
+        modelRuntime: session.modelRuntime,
+        ...(session.model
+          ? {
+              modelSelection: {
+                provider: session.model.provider,
+                modelId: session.model.id,
+              },
+            }
+          : config?.provider && config.modelId
+            ? {
+                modelSelection: {
+                  provider: config.provider,
+                  modelId: config.modelId,
+                },
+              }
+            : {}),
+        ...(config ? { config } : {}),
+        blockImages,
+        fileAttachmentPaths: collectFileAttachmentPaths(projection.messages),
+        limitations,
+      };
+    }
+
+    const entry = await this.catalogs.sessions.getSession(sessionRef);
+    if (!entry) {
+      throw new Error(`Session ${sessionKey(sessionRef)} is not in the catalog.`);
+    }
+    const workspace = await this.catalogs.workspaces.getWorkspace(sessionRef.workspaceId);
+    if (!workspace) {
+      throw new Error(`Workspace ${sessionRef.workspaceId} is not in the catalog.`);
+    }
+    const sessionFile =
+      entry.sessionFilePath ??
+      (await this.resolveSessionFilePath(sessionRef, entry));
+    if (!sessionFile) {
+      throw new Error(`Session ${sessionKey(sessionRef)} has no tracked session file.`);
+    }
+    // loadEntriesFromFile() 会在末尾缺换行时补写主文件；这里用公开的纯解析函数和
+    // in-memory manager，旧 schema 迁移只发生在内存，绝不改写来源 JSONL。
+    const content = await readFile(sessionFile, "utf8");
+    assertReadableSessionJsonl(content, sessionFile);
+    const entries: FileEntry[] = parseSessionEntries(content);
+    const header = entries[0];
+    if (header?.type !== "session" || typeof header.id !== "string") {
+      throw new Error(`来源会话日志为空或格式无效：${sessionFile}`);
+    }
+    if (header.id !== sessionRef.sessionId) {
+      throw new Error(
+        `来源会话日志与目标会话不匹配：${sessionFile}（日志 ${header.id}，目标 ${sessionRef.sessionId}）`,
+      );
+    }
+    const sessionManager = SessionManager.inMemory(workspace.path, undefined, entries);
+    const projection = sessionManager.buildSessionProjection();
+    const config = deriveSessionConfig(sessionManager);
+    const limitations: string[] = [];
+    if (entry.status === "failed") {
+      limitations.push("主对话最近一次运行失败；现有 projection 仅代表已持久化的有效材料。");
+    }
+    const modelRuntime = await ModelRuntime.create({
+      ...(this.agentDir
+        ? {
+            authPath: resolve(this.agentDir, "auth.json"),
+            modelsPath: resolve(this.agentDir, "models.json"),
+          }
+        : {}),
+      refreshOnCreate: false,
+    });
+    return {
+      ref: { ...sessionRef },
+      workspacePath: workspace.path,
+      status: entry.status,
+      boundaryId: sessionManager.getLeafId(),
+      capturedAt: nowIso(),
+      sourceBusy: false,
+      messages: projection.messages,
+      modelRuntime,
+      ...(config?.provider && config.modelId
+        ? {
+            modelSelection: {
+              provider: config.provider,
+              modelId: config.modelId,
+            },
+          }
+        : {}),
+      ...(config ? { config } : {}),
+      blockImages: false,
+      fileAttachmentPaths: collectFileAttachmentPaths(projection.messages),
+      limitations,
+    };
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Whether any running session currently uses this execution root.
+   *
+   * Runtime worktrees are selected per dispatch, so the cwd alone is not a
+   * sufficient owner. Only a session that is actively busy can still write
+   * there; idle sessions keep their runtime alive and must not block cleanup
+   * forever after the dispatch has settled.
+   */
+  isExecutionActive(executionRoot: string): boolean {
+    const requested = resolve(executionRoot);
+    for (const record of this.records.values()) {
+      if (
+        record.closed ||
+        resolve(record.workspace.path) !== requested ||
+        (record.status !== "running" &&
+          record.status !== "stopping" &&
+          !record.sendInFlight &&
+          !record.promptStarting &&
+          !record.session?.isCompacting)
+      ) {
+        continue;
+      }
+      return true;
+    }
+    return false;
+  }

+/** 公开 parser 会跳过坏行；来源文件必须逐行完整可读，不能静默丢一段主对话。 */
+function assertReadableSessionJsonl(content: string, sessionFile: string): void {
+  if (!content.trim()) {
+    throw new Error(`来源会话日志为空：${sessionFile}`);
+  }
+  for (const [index, line] of content.split(/\r?\n/).entries()) {
+    if (!line.trim()) continue;
+    try {
+      JSON.parse(line);
+    } catch {
+      throw new Error(
+        `来源会话日志第 ${index + 1} 行不是有效 JSON，拒绝以缺失上下文继续：${sessionFile}`,
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

+async function setupPaoInventory(t: test.TestContext) {
+  const { agentDir, workspacePath } = await createDirs();
+  const paoHome = join(agentDir, "pao-home");
+  const env = {
+    PAO_HOME: paoHome,
+    PAO_CODING_AGENT_DIR: agentDir,
+    PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR: agentDir,
+    PAO_DISABLE_BUILTIN_EXTENSION: "0",
+    PAO_ROLE: "主控",
+    PI_OFFLINE: "1",
+  };
+  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(env)) {
+    const previous = process.env[key];
+    process.env[key] = value;
+    t.after(() => {
+      if (previous === undefined) delete process.env[key];
+      else process.env[key] = previous;
+    });
+  }
+  const network = t.mock.method(globalThis, "fetch", async () => {

+    await reload.call(this);

+  });

+    inspect,
+    inventory: () => {

+      return loaded;
+    },
+  };

+await test("PAO inventory respects include, environment and extensions switches", async (t) => {
+  const fixture = await setupPaoInventory(t);
+  const options = {
+    agentDir: fixture.agentDir,
+    piAddons: [],
+    builtinExtensions: [DEMO],
+    paoExtensionOptions: fixture.paoExtensionOptions,
+  };
+  const supervisor = new RuntimeSupervisor(options);
+  t.after(() => supervisor.disposeAll());
+  const hasPao = () => fixture.inventory().extensions.some((entry) => entry.path === "builtin:pao");
+  await supervisor.getRuntimeSnapshot(fixture.workspace);
+  assert.equal(hasPao(), true);
+  await writeFile(
+    join(fixture.agentDir, "settings.json"),
+    JSON.stringify({ extensions: ["-builtin:pao"] }),
+  );
+  const disabled = await supervisor.refreshRuntime(fixture.workspace);
+  assert.equal(hasPao(), false);
+  assert.ok(disabled.extensions.some((entry) => entry.path === "<inline:pi-gui-demo>"));
+  await writeFile(
+    join(fixture.agentDir, "settings.json"),
+    JSON.stringify({ extensions: ["+builtin:pao"] }),
+  );
+  await supervisor.refreshRuntime(fixture.workspace);
+  assert.equal(hasPao(), true);
+
+  for (const include of [false, true]) {
+    process.env.PAO_DISABLE_BUILTIN_EXTENSION = include ? "1" : "0";
+    const gated = new RuntimeSupervisor({ ...options, includePaoBuiltinExtension: include });
+    t.after(() => gated.disposeAll());
+    await gated.getRuntimeSnapshot(fixture.workspace);
+    assert.equal(hasPao(), false);
+  }
+});

+/** 追加提供方模型模式，保留用户已有顺序，并避免现有项或追加项重复。 */
+export function mergeEnabledModelPatterns(
+  existingPatterns: readonly string[],
+  providerPatterns: readonly string[],
+): readonly string[] {
+  const merged = [...existingPatterns];
+  const seen = new Set(existingPatterns);
+  for (const pattern of providerPatterns) {
+    if (seen.has(pattern)) {
+      continue;
+    }
+    seen.add(pattern);
+    merged.push(pattern);
+  }
+  return merged;
+}

   readonly providers: readonly RuntimeProviderRecord[];
